*This document provides an exhaustive, categorized triage table of the **872 active browser tabs** open across all 6 Chromium windows on Tara (PID 827144). Each section details the recommended action and explicit justification for closing or keeping.*

---

## 🧭 Executive Summary of Triage Actions

| Category / Domain | Tab Count | Recommended Action | Primary Justification |
| :--- | :---: | :--- | :--- |
| **1. Auth & Login Redirects** | ~35 tabs | **Close All** | Completed OAuth sign-in and session switcher URLs. |
| **2. Completed Checkouts & Gateways** | ~8 tabs | **Close All** | Single-use 3D-Secure challenges and order receipts. |
| **3. Google Keep Search Fragments** | ~7 tabs | **Close All** | Interrupted keystroke fragments from in-flight typing. |
| **4. Ephemeral Searches & Local Maps** | ~20 tabs | **Close All** | One-off searches (H&M, Transa, Sunnyvale) already acted upon. |
| **5. Agora Node Offload** | ~225 tabs | **Archive & Close** | Every node is already in `~/garden`. The filesystem is source of truth. |
| **6. Hardware Comparison (ASUS NUC)** | ~65 tabs | **Archive Spec & Close** | Mini-PC research across Digitec/Galaxus preserved here in table. |
| **7. Wikipedia Reading Queue** | ~38 tabs | **Bookmark & Close** | Reading trail cataloged here; no need to keep active DOM in RAM. |
| **8. Exact Duplicate Tabs** | ~20 tabs | **Close Duplicates** | Multiple copies of Beca Flancia, YouTube home, Keep, Gemini. |
| **9. Living Keepers (Must Stay Open)** | ~100 tabs | **KEEP OPEN** | In-flight writing (Beca Flancia), Overleaf papers, Zulip, Mastodon, AI. |
